    52	class ModelRequestBarrierTests(unittest.TestCase):
    53	    """AC-05: one test per named barrier -- the requested model is denied, the denial
    54	    names the barrier, and a genuinely eligible alternative still wins. Nothing here
    55	    ever asserts through `set_agents_app._decide_status`/the CLI envelope's ok/exit
    56	    classification -- that helper's `_DECIDE_OK_NON_EXECUTABLE_REASONS` closed-tuple
    57	    check (routing_cli.py) only special-cases `RUNTIME_REDIRECTED`/`BILLING_RANK `
    58	    prefixes, not `MODEL_PINNED`/`MODEL_PIN_UNAVAILABLE` (pre-existing, ADR-0032) nor
    59	    this AC's own `MODEL_REQUEST_APPLIED`/`MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE` markers -- a
    60	    VERIFIED review decision carrying either marker can misclassify as ok=false at the
    61	    CLI layer today. That gap predates this package, is out of this package's ALCANCE
    62	    (routing_cli.py is not a listed file), and is flagged here rather than silently
    63	    inherited into a passing test: every assertion below reads `RouteDecision` fields
    64	    directly (`.provider`, `.model`, `.exclusions`, `.reason_codes`,
    65	    `.independence_verified`), never the CLI's ok/exit computation."""
    66	
    67	    def _service(self, snapshot, inventory, td):
    68	        return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(
    69	            snapshot, _ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(Path(td) / "state"))
    70	
    71	    def test_review_provider_conflict_barrier_denies_the_requested_model(self):
    72	        # "El usuario pidió poder elegir, no poder saltear": a model sharing the
    73	        # writer's PROVIDER is exactly what reviewer independence forbids -- requesting
    74	        # it explicitly must still be denied.
    75	        writer_route = _route("anthropic", "opus", "frontier", "opus")
    76	        same_provider_reviewer = _route("anthropic", "sonnet", "frontier", "sonnet")
    77	        alt_reviewer = _route("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code", "frontier", "kimi")
    78	        identities = frozenset(
    79	            (r.route_id, "opencode", r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort)
    80	            for r in (writer_route, same_provider_reviewer, alt_reviewer))
    81	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, same_provider_reviewer, alt_reviewer), identities)
    82	        inventory = {("opencode", "anthropic"): {"opus", "sonnet"}, ("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {"kimi-k2.7-code"}}
    83	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
    84	            svc = self._service(snapshot, inventory, td)
    85	            writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
    86	                               svc._observe_for_invocation(**_facts("implementer", "opencode")))
    87	            self.assertTrue(writer.execution_enabled, writer.reason_codes)
    88	            svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id); svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, "success")
    89	            review_facts = svc._observe_for_invocation(
    90	                **_facts("package-reviewer", "opencode", operation="inspection", read_write="read"))
    91	            review = svc.route(
    92	                routing.TaskRequest("package-reviewer", "inspection", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
    93	                review_facts, review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=("anthropic", "sonnet"))
    94	        # Denied: the requested identity never wins.
    95	        self.assertNotEqual((review.provider, review.model), ("anthropic", "sonnet"))
    96	        self.assertEqual((review.provider, review.model), ("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code"))
    97	        self.assertTrue(review.independence_verified)
    98	        # The exclusion carries the barrier's own name.
    99	        self.assertIn({"route_id": same_provider_reviewer.route_id, "reason": "REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT"},
   100	                      review.exclusions)
   101	        # AC-06: the decision's own reason_codes name the requested model and why.
   102	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE requested=anthropic/sonnet reason=REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT",
   103	                      review.reason_codes)
   104	
   105	    def test_review_model_conflict_barrier_denies_the_requested_model(self):
   106	        # Same underlying model, different provider spelling (the real, documented
   107	        # anthropic/opus <-> opencode-zen/claude-opus-4-8 alias, `catalog.canonical_model`)
   108	        # -- REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT, the layer-2 defense-in-depth exclusion (repair SEC-001).
   109	        writer_route = _route("anthropic", "opus", "frontier", "opus")
   110	        alias_reviewer = _route("opencode-zen", "claude-opus-4-8", "frontier", "claude-opus")
   111	        alt_reviewer = _route("opencode-go", "kimi-k3", "frontier", "kimi")
   112	        identities = frozenset(
   113	            (r.route_id, "opencode", r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort)
   114	            for r in (writer_route, alias_reviewer, alt_reviewer))
   115	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, alias_reviewer, alt_reviewer), identities)
   116	        inventory = {("opencode", "anthropic"): {"opus"}, ("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {"claude-opus-4-8"},
   117	                     ("opencode", "opencode-go"): {"kimi-k3"}}
   118	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   119	            svc = self._service(snapshot, inventory, td)
   120	            writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
   121	                               svc._observe_for_invocation(**_facts("implementer", "opencode")))
   122	            self.assertTrue(writer.execution_enabled, writer.reason_codes)
   123	            svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id); svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, "success")
   124	            review_facts = svc._observe_for_invocation(
   125	                **_facts("package-reviewer", "opencode", operation="inspection", read_write="read"))

   127	                routing.TaskRequest("package-reviewer", "inspection", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),

   129	        self.assertNotEqual((review.provider, review.model), ("opencode-zen", "claude-opus-4-8"))
   130	        self.assertEqual((review.provider, review.model), ("opencode-go", "kimi-k3"))
   131	        self.assertTrue(review.independence_verified)
   132	        self.assertIn({"route_id": alias_reviewer.route_id, "reason": "REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT"}, review.exclusions)
   133	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE requested=opencode-zen/claude-opus-4-8 reason=REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT",
   134	                      review.reason_codes)

   136	    def test_tier_insufficient_barrier_denies_the_requested_model(self):
   137	        # `implementation`+`low` risk requires `balanced` (routing_core.domain.required_tier).
   138	        # The requested candidate only reaches `fast` -- TIER_INSUFFICIENT.
   139	        fast_candidate = _route("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-sol", "fast", "gpt-5.6", roles=("implementer",))
   140	        balanced_candidate = _route("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code", "balanced", "kimi", roles=("implementer",))
   141	        identities = frozenset(
   142	            (r.route_id, "opencode", r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort)
   143	            for r in (fast_candidate, balanced_candidate))
   144	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast_candidate, balanced_candidate), identities)
   145	        inventory = {("opencode", "openai-codex"): {"gpt-5.6-sol"}, ("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {"kimi-k2.7-code"}}
   146	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   147	            svc = self._service(snapshot, inventory, td)

   149	            decision = svc.route(
   150	                routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "implementation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
   151	                facts, model_request=("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-sol"))
   152	        self.assertTrue(decision.execution_enabled, decision.reason_codes)
   153	        self.assertNotEqual((decision.provider, decision.model), ("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-sol"))
   154	        self.assertEqual((decision.provider, decision.model), ("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code"))
   155	        self.assertIn({"route_id": fast_candidate.route_id, "reason": "TIER_INSUFFICIENT"}, decision.exclusions)
   156	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE requested=openai-codex/gpt-5.6-sol reason=TIER_INSUFFICIENT",
   157	                      decision.reason_codes)

   159	    def test_unaudited_pair_barrier_denies_the_requested_model(self):
   160	        # "Par no auditado": the (runtime, provider) pair the requested candidate would
   161	        # need is never in `_PAIR_COMMANDS`/never authenticated -- `route()` cannot
   162	        # structurally distinguish "genuinely never audited" from "audited but not
   163	        # currently authenticated"; both surface as PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED, which is
   164	        # the honest, accurate reason either way.
   165	        eligible = _route("anthropic", "opus", "balanced", "opus", roles=("implementer",))
   166	        unaudited = _route("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code", "balanced", "kimi", roles=("implementer",))
   167	        identities = frozenset(
   168	            (r.route_id, "claude-code", r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort)
   169	            for r in (eligible, unaudited))
   170	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), identities)
   171	        # The opencode-zen pair is simply absent -- never probed, never authenticated.
   172	        inventory = {("claude-code", "anthropic"): {"opus"}}
   173	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   174	            svc = self._service(snapshot, inventory, td)
   175	            facts = svc._observe_for_invocation(**_facts("implementer", "claude-code", task_class="implementation"))
   176	            decision = svc.route(
   177	                routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "implementation", selected_runtime="claude-code"),
   178	                facts, model_request=("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code"))
   179	        self.assertTrue(decision.execution_enabled, decision.reason_codes)
   180	        self.assertNotEqual((decision.provider, decision.model), ("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code"))
   181	        self.assertEqual((decision.provider, decision.model), ("anthropic", "opus"))
   182	        self.assertIn({"route_id": unaudited.route_id, "reason": "PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED"}, decision.exclusions)
   183	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE requested=opencode-zen/kimi-k2.7-code reason=PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED",
   184	                      decision.reason_codes)

   186	    def test_catalog_ceiling_barrier_denies_a_model_outside_the_curated_set(self):
   187	        # "Techo de catalogo" (resolve_ceiling, 022/P2): a model outside the curated
   188	        # ceiling never becomes a `StaticRoute` at all (`catalog.build_snapshot`) -- by
   189	        # the time `route()` runs, that is observably identical to "not in the
   190	        # catalog", which is exactly what this test proves against the REAL,
   191	        # production catalog (`ai/catalogs/routes.v1.toml` + `models.toml`), not a
   192	        # synthetic snapshot -- "opencode-zen" is a real, valid provider (so this is
   193	        # not simply another unaudited-pair case), the specific model id just never
   194	        # was curated.
   195	        config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / "models.toml")
   196	        inventory = {("codex", "openai-codex"): set(config["catalog"]["codex"])}
   197	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   198	            svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, _ROSTER, inventory, Path(td) / "state", simulate=False)
   199	            facts = svc._observe_for_invocation(**_facts("implementer", "codex", task_class="mechanical"))
   200	            decision = svc.route(
   201	                routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "mechanical", selected_runtime="codex"),
   202	                facts, model_request=("opencode-zen", "definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz"))
   203	        self.assertTrue(decision.execution_enabled, decision.reason_codes)
   204	        self.assertNotEqual((decision.provider, decision.model),
   205	                            ("opencode-zen", "definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz"))
   206	        self.assertIn(
   207	            "MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE requested=opencode-zen/definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz reason=NOT_IN_CATALOG",
   208	            decision.reason_codes)

   210	    def test_model_request_applied_when_the_requested_model_is_genuinely_eligible(self):
   211	        # Positive control: two same-tier, same-billing-kind candidates that would
   212	        # otherwise tie-break on `curated_priority` (route_a wins by default);
   213	        # requesting route_b flips the winner -- proving `model_request` really is a
   214	        # live sort factor, not dead code.
   215	        route_a = _route("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-sol", "balanced", "gpt-5.6", roles=("implementer",), curated_priority=1)
   216	        route_b = _route("opencode-go", "kimi-k3", "balanced", "kimi", roles=("implementer",), curated_priority=5)
   217	        identities = frozenset(
   218	            (r.route_id, "opencode", r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (route_a, route_b))
   219	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((route_a, route_b), identities)
   220	        inventory = {("opencode", "openai-codex"): {"gpt-5.6-sol"}, ("opencode", "opencode-go"): {"kimi-k3"}}
   221	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   222	            svc = self._service(snapshot, inventory, td)
   223	            facts = svc._observe_for_invocation(**_facts("implementer", "opencode", task_class="implementation"))
   224	            default = svc.route(
   225	                routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "implementation", selected_runtime="opencode"), facts)
   226	        self.assertEqual((default.provider, default.model), ("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-sol"))
   227	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   228	            svc = self._service(snapshot, inventory, td)
   229	            facts = svc._observe_for_invocation(**_facts("implementer", "opencode", task_class="implementation"))
   230	            biased = svc.route(
   231	                routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "implementation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
   232	                facts, model_request=("opencode-go", "kimi-k3"))
   233	        self.assertEqual((biased.provider, biased.model), ("opencode-go", "kimi-k3"))
   234	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_APPLIED opencode-go/kimi-k3", biased.reason_codes)
   235	
   236	    def test_model_request_outranked_when_eligible_but_a_lower_tier_still_wins(self):
   237	        # AC-06's third bucket: the requested candidate is NOT hard-excluded (it is a
   238	        # genuinely eligible route, unlike the 5 barrier tests above) but a DIFFERENT,
   239	        # lower-tier candidate still wins -- tier ordering predates this AC and
   240	        # `model_request` never outranks it (`service.py` sort key: TIER_ORDER sits
   241	        # BEFORE the new factor). "Eligible" is not the same as "chosen", and this is
   242	        # still named, never silent.
   243	        fast_candidate = _route("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-sol", "fast", "gpt-5.6", roles=("implementer",))
   244	        balanced_candidate = _route("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code", "balanced", "kimi", roles=("implementer",))
   245	        identities = frozenset(
   246	            (r.route_id, "opencode", r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort)
   247	            for r in (fast_candidate, balanced_candidate))
   248	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast_candidate, balanced_candidate), identities)
   249	        inventory = {("opencode", "openai-codex"): {"gpt-5.6-sol"}, ("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {"kimi-k2.7-code"}}
   250	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   251	            svc = self._service(snapshot, inventory, td)
   252	            # `documentation`+`low` needs only `fast` (routing_core.domain.required_tier)
   253	            # -- BOTH candidates are eligible (a higher tier always satisfies a lower
   254	            # floor), so `balanced_candidate` is not hard-excluded here.
   255	            facts = svc._observe_for_invocation(**_facts("implementer", "opencode", task_class="documentation"))
   256	            decision = svc.route(
   257	                routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
   258	                facts, model_request=("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code"))
   259	        self.assertNotIn({"route_id": balanced_candidate.route_id, "reason": "TIER_INSUFFICIENT"}, decision.exclusions)
   260	        self.assertEqual((decision.provider, decision.model), ("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-sol"))
   261	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE requested=opencode-zen/kimi-k2.7-code reason=OUTRANKED",
   262	                      decision.reason_codes)
   263	
   264	    def test_model_request_enters_after_the_exclusion_loop_never_before_it(self):
   265	        # The sharpest, most direct proof of AC-05's central claim: for the SAME
   266	        # scenario, the exclusion list (and the winner) is BYTE-IDENTICAL with and
   267	        # without `model_request` set to a value that violates a hard exclusion --
   268	        # the preference never touches the exclusion loop's own output, only the
   269	        # sort ordering of whatever the loop already let through.
   270	        writer_route = _route("anthropic", "opus", "frontier", "opus")
   271	        same_provider_reviewer = _route("anthropic", "sonnet", "frontier", "sonnet")
   272	        alt_reviewer = _route("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code", "frontier", "kimi")
   273	        identities = frozenset(
   274	            (r.route_id, "opencode", r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort)
   275	            for r in (writer_route, same_provider_reviewer, alt_reviewer))
   276	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, same_provider_reviewer, alt_reviewer), identities)
   277	        inventory = {("opencode", "anthropic"): {"opus", "sonnet"}, ("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {"kimi-k2.7-code"}}
   278	
   279	        def _review(model_request):
   280	            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   281	                svc = self._service(snapshot, inventory, td)
   282	                writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
   283	                                   svc._observe_for_invocation(**_facts("implementer", "opencode")))
   284	                svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id); svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, "success")
   285	                review_facts = svc._observe_for_invocation(
   286	                    **_facts("package-reviewer", "opencode", operation="inspection", read_write="read"))
   287	                return svc.route(
   288	                    routing.TaskRequest("package-reviewer", "inspection", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
   289	                    review_facts, review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=model_request)
   290	
   291	        without_request = _review(None)
   292	        with_request = _review(("anthropic", "sonnet"))  # the violating identity, explicitly requested
   293	        # Same excluded set (the barrier reasons are untouched by the preference)...
   294	        self.assertEqual(without_request.exclusions, with_request.exclusions)
   295	        # ...and the same winner (no bypass: the preference could not smuggle the
   296	        # violating identity past the exclusion loop it never runs before).
   297	        self.assertEqual((without_request.provider, without_request.model),
   298	                         (with_request.provider, with_request.model))
   299	        # The only observable difference is the additive, honest "why not" marker.
   300	        self.assertFalse(any(c.startswith("MODEL_REQUEST_") for c in without_request.reason_codes))
   301	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE requested=anthropic/sonnet reason=REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT",
   302	                      with_request.reason_codes)
   303

   311	                         ("openai-codex", "gpt-5.6-sol"))

   315	                    "openai-codex/", "/gpt-5.6-sol", 5, None, [], {"provider": "openai-codex"}):

   319	
   320	class ModelRequestCliTests(unittest.TestCase):
   321	    """AC-04 (closed key set, end to end via the real CLI) / AC-06 (the marker reaches
   322	    the JSON envelope) / AC-07 (ephemeral: nothing written, nothing leaks into the next
   323	    decision) -- writer-role scenarios throughout, deliberately sidestepping the
   324	    pre-existing `_decide_status` review-role gap documented on
   325	    `ModelRequestBarrierTests` above (out of this package's ALCANCE)."""
   326	
   327	    def _probe_stubs(self, td):
   328	        # Same fixture shape as tests/test_routing.py's `_probe_stubs`: codex/claude
   329	        # report authenticated, opencode lists exactly `openai/gpt-5.6-sol`.
   330	        bins = Path(td) / "bin"; bins.mkdir(); log = Path(td) / "probes.log"
   331	        scripts = {
   332	            "codex": '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho "Logged in using ChatGPT" 1>&2\n' % log,
   333	            "claude": '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho \'{"loggedIn": true}\'\n' % log,
   334	            "opencode": ('#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\n'
   335	                         'if [ "$1" = "auth" ]; then printf "\\342\\227\\217  OpenAI oauth\\n"; exit 0; fi\n'
   336	                         'if [ "$2" = "openai" ]; then echo "openai/gpt-5.6-sol"; exit 0; fi\n'
   337	                         'echo "Error: Provider not found: $2"; exit 0\n') % log,
   338	        }
   339	        for name, body in scripts.items():
   340	            path = bins / name; path.write_text(body); path.chmod(0o755)
   341	        return bins, log
   342	
   343	    def _cli_env(self, routing_root, state_dir, bins=None):
   344	        env = dict(os.environ)
   345	        env["SET_AGENTS_ROUTING_TEST_ROOT"] = str(routing_root)
   346	        env["SET_AGENTS_STATE"] = str(state_dir)  # AC-07: redirects MODEL_PREFERENCE_PATH too
   347	        if bins is not None: env["PATH"] = f"{bins}:{env['PATH']}"
   348	        return env
   349	
   350	    def _cli_run(self, args, env, input_text=None):
   351	        return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py", *args],
   352	                              cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True, env=env, input=input_text)
   353	
   354	    def test_unknown_key_still_rejected_after_model_request_joins_the_allowed_set(self):
   355	        # AC-04: the closed set gained exactly ONE key -- an unrelated bogus key is
   356	        # still a PARSE failure, exit 2. Fails before any catalog/probe access, so no
   357	        # stub bins/temp roots are needed at all.
   358	        env = dict(os.environ)
   359	        descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical", "bogus_key": "x"})
   360	        result = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, descriptor)
   361	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, (result.stdout, result.stderr))
   362	        self.assertIn("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID", result.stdout)
   363	
   364	    def test_malformed_model_request_value_is_a_parse_error_not_a_silent_degrade(self):
   365	        env = dict(os.environ)
   366	        for bad in ("openai-codex", "not-a-real-provider/gpt-5.6-sol", "openai-codex/bad model!"):
   367	            descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical", "model_request": bad})
   368	            result = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, descriptor)
   369	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, (bad, result.stdout, result.stderr))
   370	            self.assertIn("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID", result.stdout)
   371	
   372	    def test_valid_model_request_key_flows_end_to_end_and_names_itself_when_applied(self):
   373	        # The catalog's own default winner for mechanical+codex is openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna
   374	        # (curated_priority 10); anthropic/haiku is a second, genuinely eligible fast-tier
   375	        # candidate (curated_priority 20) -- requesting it by name must flip the pick.
   376	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   377	            bins, _log = self._probe_stubs(td)
   378	            env = self._cli_env(Path(td) / "routing-root", Path(td) / "state", bins)
   379	            descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical",
   380	                                     "selected_runtime": "codex", "model_request": "anthropic/haiku"})
   381	            result = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, descriptor)
   382	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, (result.stdout, result.stderr))
   383	        data = json.loads(result.stdout)["data"]
   384	        self.assertTrue(data["execution_enabled"])
   385	        self.assertEqual((data["provider"], data["model"]), ("anthropic", "haiku"))
   386	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_APPLIED anthropic/haiku", data["reason_codes"])
   387	
   388	    def test_model_request_never_writes_model_preference_toml(self):
   389	        # AC-07: ephemeral -- the sibling preference file never even gets CREATED by a
   390	        # `--route-decide` call carrying `model_request`.
   391	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   392	            bins, _log = self._probe_stubs(td)
   393	            state_dir = Path(td) / "state"
   394	            env = self._cli_env(Path(td) / "routing-root", state_dir, bins)
   395	            descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical",
   396	                                     "selected_runtime": "codex", "model_request": "openai-codex/gpt-5.6-sol"})
   397	            result = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, descriptor)
   398	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, (result.stdout, result.stderr))
   399	            self.assertFalse((state_dir / "model-preference.toml").exists())
   400	
   401	    def test_model_request_does_not_bias_a_later_decide_call_without_it(self):
   402	        # AC-07: never alters the persistent pin/preference state a LATER decision
   403	        # (same process lifetime, same routing root) reads -- the baseline winner
   404	        # (openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna) before and after an intervening call that
   405	        # requests, and gets, the DIFFERENT eligible anthropic/haiku identity is
   406	        # unchanged.
   407	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   408	            bins, _log = self._probe_stubs(td)
   409	            env = self._cli_env(Path(td) / "routing-root", Path(td) / "state", bins)
   410	            plain_descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical",
   411	                                           "selected_runtime": "codex"})
   412	            baseline = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, plain_descriptor)
   413	            self.assertEqual(baseline.returncode, 0, (baseline.stdout, baseline.stderr))
   414	            baseline_model = json.loads(baseline.stdout)["data"]["model"]
   415	            requested_descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical",
   416	                                               "selected_runtime": "codex",
   417	                                               "model_request": "anthropic/haiku"})
   418	            biased = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, requested_descriptor)
   419	            self.assertEqual(biased.returncode, 0, (biased.stdout, biased.stderr))
   420	            self.assertEqual(json.loads(biased.stdout)["data"]["model"], "haiku")
   421	            after = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, plain_descriptor)
   422	            self.assertEqual(after.returncode, 0, (after.stdout, after.stderr))
   423	            self.assertEqual(json.loads(after.stdout)["data"]["model"], baseline_model)
   424	            self.assertNotEqual(baseline_model, "haiku")

   428	    unittest.main()

     2	per-instance `model_request`.
     3	
     4	New tests only, additive alongside the existing immutable suites. This file proves the
     5	central AC-05 claim directly, service-level: `model_request` is a soft SORT factor that
     6	runs strictly AFTER the hard-exclusion loop (`RoutingService.route`, service.py) --
     7	never before it, never able to widen the candidate set past a hard barrier
     8	(REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT, REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT, TIER_INSUFFICIENT,
     9	PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED for a never-audited pair, or a model simply absent from the
    10	curated catalog/ceiling). One test per barrier (AC-05), a positive control proving the
    11	preference DOES move the order among genuinely eligible candidates, an explicit
    12	before/after-the-exclusion-loop comparison, AC-04's closed descriptor-key set, AC-06's
    13	named "why not" reason code, and AC-07's ephemerality (never written to
    14	`model-preference.toml`, never leaks into a later decision).

    33	_ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / "roles.tsv")

    38	def _route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=_FULL_ROLES, effort="medium", curated_priority=1):
    39	    rid = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, _TOOLS, curated_priority)
    40	    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, _TOOLS, curated_priority, rid)

    42	
    43	def _facts(role, runtime, **changes):
    44	    data = dict(role=role, operation="change", task_class="documentation", read_write="write",
    45	                write_started=False, risk="low", criticality="", affected_surfaces=(),
    46	                required_tools=("read",), context_required=True, context_present=True,
    47	                critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
    48	    data.update(changes)
    49	    return data

    52	class ModelRequestBarrierTests(unittest.TestCase):
    53	    """AC-05: one test per named barrier -- the requested model is denied, the denial
    54	    names the barrier, and a genuinely eligible alternative still wins. Nothing here
    55	    ever asserts through `set_agents_app._decide_status`/the CLI envelope's ok/exit
    56	    classification -- that helper's `_DECIDE_OK_NON_EXECUTABLE_REASONS` closed-tuple
    57	    check (routing_cli.py) only special-cases `RUNTIME_REDIRECTED`/`BILLING_RANK `
    58	    prefixes, not `MODEL_PINNED`/`MODEL_PIN_UNAVAILABLE` (pre-existing, ADR-0032) nor
    59	    this AC's own `MODEL_REQUEST_APPLIED`/`MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE` markers -- a
    60	    VERIFIED review decision carrying either marker can misclassify as ok=false at the
    61	    CLI layer today. That gap predates this package, is out of this package's ALCANCE
    62	    (routing_cli.py is not a listed file), and is flagged here rather than silently
    63	    inherited into a passing test: every assertion below reads `RouteDecision` fields
    64	    directly (`.provider`, `.model`, `.exclusions`, `.reason_codes`,
    65	    `.independence_verified`), never the CLI's ok/exit computation."""
    66	
    67	    def _service(self, snapshot, inventory, td):
    68	        return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(
    69	            snapshot, _ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(Path(td) / "state"))
    70	
    71	    def test_review_provider_conflict_barrier_denies_the_requested_model(self):
    72	        # "El usuario pidió poder elegir, no poder saltear": a model sharing the
    73	        # writer's PROVIDER is exactly what reviewer independence forbids -- requesting
    74	        # it explicitly must still be denied.
    75	        writer_route = _route("anthropic", "opus", "frontier", "opus")
    76	        same_provider_reviewer = _route("anthropic", "sonnet", "frontier", "sonnet")
    77	        alt_reviewer = _route("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code", "frontier", "kimi")
    78	        identities = frozenset(
    79	            (r.route_id, "opencode", r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort)
    80	            for r in (writer_route, same_provider_reviewer, alt_reviewer))
    81	        snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, same_provider_reviewer, alt_reviewer), identities)
    82	        inventory = {("opencode", "anthropic"): {"opus", "sonnet"}, ("opencode", "opencode-zen"): {"kimi-k2.7-code"}}
    83	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
    84	            svc = self._service(snapshot, inventory, td)
    85	            writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
    86	                               svc._observe_for_invocation(**_facts("implementer", "opencode")))
    87	            self.assertTrue(writer.execution_enabled, writer.reason_codes)
    88	            svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id); svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, "success")
    89	            review_facts = svc._observe_for_invocation(
    90	                **_facts("package-reviewer", "opencode", operation="inspection", read_write="read"))
    91	            review = svc.route(
    92	                routing.TaskRequest("package-reviewer", "inspection", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode"),
    93	                review_facts, review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=("anthropic", "sonnet"))
    94	        # Denied: the requested identity never wins.
    95	        self.assertNotEqual((review.provider, review.model), ("anthropic", "sonnet"))
    96	        self.assertEqual((review.provider, review.model), ("opencode-zen", "kimi-k2.7-code"))
    97	        self.assertTrue(review.independence_verified)
    98	        # The exclusion carries the barrier's own name.
    99	        self.assertIn({"route_id": same_provider_reviewer.route_id, "reason": "REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT"},
   100	                      review.exclusions)
   101	        # AC-06: the decision's own reason_codes name the requested model and why.
   102	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE requested=anthropic/sonnet reason=REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT",
   103	                      review.reason_codes)

   320	class ModelRequestCliTests(unittest.TestCase):
   321	    """AC-04 (closed key set, end to end via the real CLI) / AC-06 (the marker reaches
   322	    the JSON envelope) / AC-07 (ephemeral: nothing written, nothing leaks into the next
   323	    decision) -- writer-role scenarios throughout, deliberately sidestepping the
   324	    pre-existing `_decide_status` review-role gap documented on
   325	    `ModelRequestBarrierTests` above (out of this package's ALCANCE)."""
   326	
   327	    def _probe_stubs(self, td):
   328	        # Same fixture shape as tests/test_routing.py's `_probe_stubs`: codex/claude
   329	        # report authenticated, opencode lists exactly `openai/gpt-5.6-sol`.
   330	        bins = Path(td) / "bin"; bins.mkdir(); log = Path(td) / "probes.log"
   331	        scripts = {
   332	            "codex": '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho "Logged in using ChatGPT" 1>&2\n' % log,
   333	            "claude": '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho \'{"loggedIn": true}\'\n' % log,
   334	            "opencode": ('#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\n'
   335	                         'if [ "$1" = "auth" ]; then printf "\\342\\227\\217  OpenAI oauth\\n"; exit 0; fi\n'
   336	                         'if [ "$2" = "openai" ]; then echo "openai/gpt-5.6-sol"; exit 0; fi\n'
   337	                         'echo "Error: Provider not found: $2"; exit 0\n') % log,
   338	        }
   339	        for name, body in scripts.items():
   340	            path = bins / name; path.write_text(body); path.chmod(0o755)
   341	        return bins, log
   342	
   343	    def _cli_env(self, routing_root, state_dir, bins=None):
   344	        env = dict(os.environ)
   345	        env["SET_AGENTS_ROUTING_TEST_ROOT"] = str(routing_root)
   346	        env["SET_AGENTS_STATE"] = str(state_dir)  # AC-07: redirects MODEL_PREFERENCE_PATH too
   347	        if bins is not None: env["PATH"] = f"{bins}:{env['PATH']}"
   348	        return env
   349	
   350	    def _cli_run(self, args, env, input_text=None):
   351	        return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py", *args],
   352	                              cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True, env=env, input=input_text)
   353	
   354	    def test_unknown_key_still_rejected_after_model_request_joins_the_allowed_set(self):
   355	        # AC-04: the closed set gained exactly ONE key -- an unrelated bogus key is
   356	        # still a PARSE failure, exit 2. Fails before any catalog/probe access, so no
   357	        # stub bins/temp roots are needed at all.
   358	        env = dict(os.environ)
   359	        descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical", "bogus_key": "x"})
   360	        result = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, descriptor)
   361	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, (result.stdout, result.stderr))
   362	        self.assertIn("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID", result.stdout)
   363	
   364	    def test_malformed_model_request_value_is_a_parse_error_not_a_silent_degrade(self):
   365	        env = dict(os.environ)
   366	        for bad in ("openai-codex", "not-a-real-provider/gpt-5.6-sol", "openai-codex/bad model!"):
   367	            descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical", "model_request": bad})
   368	            result = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, descriptor)
   369	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, (bad, result.stdout, result.stderr))
   370	            self.assertIn("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID", result.stdout)

   372	    def test_valid_model_request_key_flows_end_to_end_and_names_itself_when_applied(self):
   373	        # The catalog's own default winner for mechanical+codex is openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna
   374	        # (curated_priority 10); anthropic/haiku is a second, genuinely eligible fast-tier
   375	        # candidate (curated_priority 20) -- requesting it by name must flip the pick.
   376	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   377	            bins, _log = self._probe_stubs(td)
   378	            env = self._cli_env(Path(td) / "routing-root", Path(td) / "state", bins)
   379	            descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical",
   380	                                     "selected_runtime": "codex", "model_request": "anthropic/haiku"})
   381	            result = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, descriptor)
   382	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, (result.stdout, result.stderr))
   383	        data = json.loads(result.stdout)["data"]
   384	        self.assertTrue(data["execution_enabled"])
   385	        self.assertEqual((data["provider"], data["model"]), ("anthropic", "haiku"))
   386	        self.assertIn("MODEL_REQUEST_APPLIED anthropic/haiku", data["reason_codes"])
   387	
   388	    def test_model_request_never_writes_model_preference_toml(self):
   389	        # AC-07: ephemeral -- the sibling preference file never even gets CREATED by a
   390	        # `--route-decide` call carrying `model_request`.
   391	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   392	            bins, _log = self._probe_stubs(td)
   393	            state_dir = Path(td) / "state"
   394	            env = self._cli_env(Path(td) / "routing-root", state_dir, bins)
   395	            descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical",
   396	                                     "selected_runtime": "codex", "model_request": "openai-codex/gpt-5.6-sol"})
   397	            result = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, descriptor)
   398	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, (result.stdout, result.stderr))
   399	            self.assertFalse((state_dir / "model-preference.toml").exists())
   400	
   401	    def test_model_request_does_not_bias_a_later_decide_call_without_it(self):
   402	        # AC-07: never alters the persistent pin/preference state a LATER decision
   403	        # (same process lifetime, same routing root) reads -- the baseline winner
   404	        # (openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna) before and after an intervening call that
   405	        # requests, and gets, the DIFFERENT eligible anthropic/haiku identity is
   406	        # unchanged.
   407	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   408	            bins, _log = self._probe_stubs(td)
   409	            env = self._cli_env(Path(td) / "routing-root", Path(td) / "state", bins)
   410	            plain_descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical",
   411	                                           "selected_runtime": "codex"})
   412	            baseline = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, plain_descriptor)
   413	            self.assertEqual(baseline.returncode, 0, (baseline.stdout, baseline.stderr))
   414	            baseline_model = json.loads(baseline.stdout)["data"]["model"]
   415	            requested_descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "implementer", "task_class": "mechanical",
   416	                                               "selected_runtime": "codex",
   417	                                               "model_request": "anthropic/haiku"})
   418	            biased = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, requested_descriptor)
   419	            self.assertEqual(biased.returncode, 0, (biased.stdout, biased.stderr))
   420	            self.assertEqual(json.loads(biased.stdout)["data"]["model"], "haiku")
   421	            after = self._cli_run(["--route-decide", "-", "--json"], env, plain_descriptor)
   422	            self.assertEqual(after.returncode, 0, (after.stdout, after.stderr))
   423	            self.assertEqual(json.loads(after.stdout)["data"]["model"], baseline_model)
   424	            self.assertNotEqual(baseline_model, "haiku")
   425	
   426	
   427	if __name__ == "__main__":
   428	    unittest.main()
   625	def cmd_route_decide(source, human=False, fresh=False):
   626	    # AC-04 (026-orquestador-elige-modelo P2): `model_request` is the ONE new key -- the
   627	    # closed set stays closed, an unrecognized key stays ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID/rc=2
   628	    # exactly as before (see the `set(doc) - allowed` check below, untouched shape).
   629	    allowed = {"role", "task_class", "risk", "review_of_run_id", "selected_runtime", "feature_id",
   630	               "package_id", "model_request"}
   631	    try:
   632	        raw = sys.stdin.read() if source == "-" else Path(source).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   633	        doc = json.loads(raw)
   634	        if not isinstance(doc, dict) or set(doc) - allowed: raise ValueError
   635	        role, task_class = doc.get("role"), doc.get("task_class")
   636	        if not isinstance(role, str) or task_class not in routing.TASK_CLASSES: raise ValueError
   637	        req_risk = doc.get("risk", "low"); runtime = doc.get("selected_runtime", "opencode")
   638	        review_of = doc.get("review_of_run_id")
   639	        feature_id = doc.get("feature_id"); package_id = doc.get("package_id")
   640	        for value in (req_risk, runtime) + tuple(v for v in (review_of, feature_id, package_id) if v is not None):
   641	            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value: raise ValueError
   642	        # F01: a descriptor risk/runtime outside the closed enum is a PARSE failure (exit 2
   643	        # ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID) — it never reaches the service to degrade into FACTS_INCOMPLETE.
   644	        if req_risk not in routing.RISK_ORDER or runtime not in routing.SELECTED_RUNTIMES: raise ValueError
   645	        # AC-04: absent key -> `None` (no preference, byte-identical to every pre-AC-04
   646	        # decision); present -> validated "provider/model" -> `(provider, model)`, or this
   647	        # same PARSE-failure/rc=2 path as every other malformed descriptor field above.
   648	        model_request_raw = doc.get("model_request")
   649	        model_request = _validate_model_request(model_request_raw) if model_request_raw is not None else None
   650	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   651	        _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, "route-decide", {}, (), ("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID",)), human); return 2
   652	    try:
   653	        config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / "models.toml")
   654	        roster = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / "roles.tsv")
   655	        row = next((item for item in roster if item["role"] == role), None)
   656	        if row is None:
   657	            _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, "route-decide", {}, (), ("FACTS_INCOMPLETE",)), human); return 1
   658	        role_class = _role_class_of(row)
   659	        # AM-1 (ADR-0006): capability decides write access and tools; task_class decides criticality
   660	        # and the base risk; the descriptor risk can only RAISE (combined in the service); context
   661	        # flags derive from the active package's context pack.
   662	        writer = role_class == "writer"
   663	        criticality = task_class if task_class in routing.CRITICAL else ""
   664	        base_risk = "high" if (criticality or task_class == "incident") else "low"
   665	        needs_context = bool(criticality) or base_risk == "high" or req_risk == "high"
   666	        context_ok, resolved_feature, resolved_package = _resolve_context_pack(feature_id, package_id)
   667	        if needs_context and context_ok is None:
   668	            # F03d: the harness itself could not narrow the default resolution to exactly one
   669	            # actively-executing package — distinct from NO_ELIGIBLE_ROUTE (a real catalog
   670	            # exclusion), and distinct from a resolved-but-missing pack (plain CONTEXT_MISSING).
   671	            data = {"feature_id": resolved_feature, "package_id": resolved_package, "context_ok": None}
   672	            _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, "route-decide", data, (), ("CONTEXT_UNRESOLVED",)), human)
   673	            return 1
   674	        context_flag = bool(context_ok)
   675	        unverified_review = role_class == "review" and not review_of
   676	        simulate = not writer and not (role_class == "review" and review_of)
   677	        config = _config_with_model_preference(config, roster)
   678	        service = routing.compose(config, roster, simulate=simulate, fresh_probes=fresh,
   679	                                  store=None if simulate else _routing_store())
   680	        request = routing.TaskRequest(role=role, operation="inspection" if task_class == "inspection" else "change",
   681	                                      task_class=task_class, risk=req_risk, selected_runtime=runtime)
   682	        facts = service._observe_for_invocation(role=role, operation=request.operation, task_class=task_class,
   683	            read_write="write" if writer else "read", write_started=False,
   684	            risk=base_risk, criticality=criticality, affected_surfaces=(),
   685	            required_tools=("read", "shell", "write") if writer else ("read",),
   686	            context_required=needs_context,
   687	            context_present=context_flag, critical_coverage=context_flag, selected_runtime=runtime)
   688	        decision = service.route(request, facts, review_of, unverified_review=unverified_review,
   689	                                 model_request=model_request)
   690	        tier = next((r.tier for r in service.snapshot.routes if r.route_id == decision.route_id), None)
   691	        data = decision.to_dict(); data["tier"] = tier; data["role_class"] = role_class
   692	        # F03: the effective (feature_id, package_id, context_ok) is always in the envelope,
   693	        # even when context wasn't needed, for audit.
   694	        data["feature_id"] = resolved_feature; data["package_id"] = resolved_package; data["context_ok"] = context_flag
   695	        # ADR-0031: mint a per-decision id (parallel to run1_/proj1_) and append the whole
   696	        # decision to the sidecar log — simulate included, which is exactly the class of
   697	        # decision that previously left no durable trace (store=None above stays as-is).
   698	        decision_id = "dec1_" + secrets.token_hex(16)
   699	        data["decision_id"] = decision_id
   700	        # ADR-0032: which of the selection paths produced this identity — "pin" (a user
   701	        # [model_pin] override was selected) or "dynamic" (the router's own pick; a
   702	        # configured-but-ineligible pin shows up as MODEL_PIN_UNAVAILABLE in
   703	        # reason_codes and still counts as dynamic). The THIRD path, the curated static
   704	        # fallback, only exists at materialization time and is recorded on the spawn
   705	        # record as MODEL_STATIC_FALLBACK (ADR-0030), never here.
   706	        selection_path = ("pin" if any(code.startswith("MODEL_PINNED") for code in decision.reason_codes)
   707	                          else "dynamic")
   708	        data["selection_path"] = selection_path
   709	        ok, exit_code = _decide_status(decision)
   710	        _append_decision_log({

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+
+config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / 'models.toml')
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
+    print_decision('CATALOG_CEILING', decision)

+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)

+import sys

+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value

+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+

diff --git a//tmp/model_request_barrier_probe.py b//tmp/model_request_barrier_probe.py

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+
+config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / 'models.toml')
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
+    print_decision('CATALOG_CEILING', decision)

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+
+config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / 'models.toml')
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
+    print_decision('CATALOG_CEILING', decision)

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+
+config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / 'models.toml')
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
+    print_decision('CATALOG_CEILING', decision)

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+
+config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / 'models.toml')
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
+    print_decision('CATALOG_CEILING', decision)
