+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+
+config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / 'models.toml')
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
+    print_decision('CATALOG_CEILING', decision)

+def _validate_model_request(value, valid_providers=_MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS):
+    """AC-04 (026-orquestador-elige-modelo P2): validates `--route-decide`'s descriptor
+    `model_request` value -- the SAME "provider/model" shape and closed, network-free
+    provider vocabulary `_validate_model_pin_entry` already enforces for the PERSISTENT
+    `[model_pin]` table (reused here as a bare value: no role key, no file, nothing
+    written -- AC-07's ephemerality lives entirely in the caller never calling
+    `atomic_write`/`MODEL_PREFERENCE_PATH` for this value, not in this function).
+    Raises plain `ValueError` (never `ModelPreferenceError`, which is the sibling
+    FILE's own exception type) -- `cmd_route_decide`'s existing `except (OSError,
+    ValueError)` clause turns it into `ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID`/rc=2, the same PARSE-time
+    fail-closed discipline as `risk`/`selected_runtime`'s enum checks (F01): a malformed
+    model_request never reaches the service to degrade silently into a different
+    reason code. Returns `(provider, model)`."""
+    if not isinstance(value, str) or value.count("/") != 1:
+        raise ValueError
+    provider, model = value.split("/", 1)
+    if provider not in valid_providers or not _MODEL_PIN_MODEL_RE.fullmatch(model):
+        raise ValueError
+    return provider, model

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+

+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))

+        print_decision(name, review)
+

+

+

+import json
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    base = Path(td)
+    bins = base / 'bin'
+    bins.mkdir()
+    log = base / 'probes.log'
+    scripts = {
+        'codex': '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho "Logged in using ChatGPT" 1>&2\n' % log,
+        'claude': '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho \'{"loggedIn": true}\'\n' % log,
+        'opencode': ('#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\n'
+                     'if [ "$1" = "auth" ]; then printf "\\342\\227\\217  OpenAI oauth\\n"; exit 0; fi\n'
+                     'if [ "$2" = "openai" ]; then echo "openai/gpt-5.6-sol"; exit 0; fi\n'
+                     'echo "Error: Provider not found: $2"; exit 0\n') % log,
+    }
+    for name, body in scripts.items():
+        path = bins / name
+        path.write_text(body)
+        path.chmod(0o755)
+    state = base / 'state'
+    env = dict(os.environ, SET_AGENTS_ROUTING_TEST_ROOT=str(base / 'routing-root'),
+               SET_AGENTS_STATE=str(state), PATH=f'{bins}:{os.environ["PATH"]}')
+    def decide(model_request):
+        descriptor = json.dumps({'role': 'implementer', 'task_class': 'mechanical',
+                                 'selected_runtime': 'codex', 'model_request': model_request})
+        return subprocess.run([sys.executable, 'ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py', '--route-decide', '-', '--json'],
+                              cwd=ROOT, text=True, input=descriptor, capture_output=True, env=env)
+    first = decide('anthropic/haiku')
+    second = decide('openai-codex/gpt-5.6-sol')
+    preference = state / 'model-preference.toml'
+    print('preference_before=MISSING')
+    print('first_rc=%s' % first.returncode)
+    print(first.stdout.strip())
+    print('second_rc=%s' % second.returncode)
+    print(second.stdout.strip())
+    print('preference_after=%s' % ('PRESENT' if preference.exists() else 'MISSING'))

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+

+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
+    print_decision('CATALOG_CEILING', decision)

+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))

+

+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+

+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))

+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))

+import json
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    base = Path(td)
+    bins = base / 'bin'
+    bins.mkdir()
+    log = base / 'probes.log'
+    scripts = {
+        'codex': '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho "Logged in using ChatGPT" 1>&2\n' % log,
+        'claude': '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho \'{"loggedIn": true}\'\n' % log,
+        'opencode': ('#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\n'
+                     'if [ "$1" = "auth" ]; then printf "\\342\\227\\217  OpenAI oauth\\n"; exit 0; fi\n'
+                     'if [ "$2" = "openai" ]; then echo "openai/gpt-5.6-sol"; exit 0; fi\n'
+                     'echo "Error: Provider not found: $2"; exit 0\n') % log,
+    }
+    for name, body in scripts.items():
+        path = bins / name
+        path.write_text(body)
+        path.chmod(0o755)
+    state = base / 'state'
+    env = dict(os.environ, SET_AGENTS_ROUTING_TEST_ROOT=str(base / 'routing-root'),
+               SET_AGENTS_STATE=str(state), PATH=f'{bins}:{os.environ["PATH"]}')
+    def decide(model_request):
+        descriptor = json.dumps({'role': 'implementer', 'task_class': 'mechanical',
+                                 'selected_runtime': 'codex', 'model_request': model_request})
+        return subprocess.run([sys.executable, 'ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py', '--route-decide', '-', '--json'],
+                              cwd=ROOT, text=True, input=descriptor, capture_output=True, env=env)
+    first = decide('anthropic/haiku')
+    second = decide('openai-codex/gpt-5.6-sol')
+    preference = state / 'model-preference.toml'
+    print('preference_before=MISSING')
+    print('first_rc=%s' % first.returncode)
+    print(first.stdout.strip())
+    print('second_rc=%s' % second.returncode)
+    print(second.stdout.strip())
+    print('preference_after=%s' % ('PRESENT' if preference.exists() else 'MISSING'))

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+
+config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / 'models.toml')
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
+    print_decision('CATALOG_CEILING', decision)

+import json
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    base = Path(td)
+    bins = base / 'bin'
+    bins.mkdir()
+    log = base / 'probes.log'
+    scripts = {
+        'codex': '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho "Logged in using ChatGPT" 1>&2\n' % log,
+        'claude': '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho \'{"loggedIn": true}\'\n' % log,
+        'opencode': ('#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\n'
+                     'if [ "$1" = "auth" ]; then printf "\\342\\227\\217  OpenAI oauth\\n"; exit 0; fi\n'
+                     'if [ "$2" = "openai" ]; then echo "openai/gpt-5.6-sol"; exit 0; fi\n'
+                     'echo "Error: Provider not found: $2"; exit 0\n') % log,
+    }
+    for name, body in scripts.items():
+        path = bins / name
+        path.write_text(body)
+        path.chmod(0o755)
+    state = base / 'state'
+    env = dict(os.environ, SET_AGENTS_ROUTING_TEST_ROOT=str(base / 'routing-root'),
+               SET_AGENTS_STATE=str(state), PATH=f'{bins}:{os.environ["PATH"]}')
+    def decide(model_request):
+        descriptor = json.dumps({'role': 'implementer', 'task_class': 'mechanical',
+                                 'selected_runtime': 'codex', 'model_request': model_request})
+        return subprocess.run([sys.executable, 'ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py', '--route-decide', '-', '--json'],
+                              cwd=ROOT, text=True, input=descriptor, capture_output=True, env=env)
+    first = decide('anthropic/haiku')
+    second = decide('openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna')
+    preference = state / 'model-preference.toml'
+    print('preference_before=MISSING')
+    print('first_rc=%s' % first.returncode)
+    print(first.stdout.strip())
+    print('second_rc=%s' % second.returncode)
+    print(second.stdout.strip())
+    print('preference_after=%s' % ('PRESENT' if preference.exists() else 'MISSING'))

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+
+config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / 'models.toml')
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
+    print_decision('CATALOG_CEILING', decision)

+import json
+import os

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    base = Path(td)
+    bins = base / 'bin'
+    bins.mkdir()
+    log = base / 'probes.log'
+    scripts = {
+        'codex': '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho "Logged in using ChatGPT" 1>&2\n' % log,
+        'claude': '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho \'{"loggedIn": true}\'\n' % log,
+        'opencode': ('#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\n'
+                     'if [ "$1" = "auth" ]; then printf "\\342\\227\\217  OpenAI oauth\\n"; exit 0; fi\n'
+                     'if [ "$2" = "openai" ]; then echo "openai/gpt-5.6-sol"; exit 0; fi\n'
+                     'echo "Error: Provider not found: $2"; exit 0\n') % log,
+    }
+    for name, body in scripts.items():
+        path = bins / name
+        path.write_text(body)
+        path.chmod(0o755)
+    state = base / 'state'
+    env = dict(os.environ, SET_AGENTS_ROUTING_TEST_ROOT=str(base / 'routing-root'),
+               SET_AGENTS_STATE=str(state), PATH=f'{bins}:{os.environ["PATH"]}')
+    def decide(model_request):
+        descriptor = json.dumps({'role': 'implementer', 'task_class': 'mechanical',
+                                 'selected_runtime': 'codex', 'model_request': model_request})
+        return subprocess.run([sys.executable, 'ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py', '--route-decide', '-', '--json'],
+                              cwd=ROOT, text=True, input=descriptor, capture_output=True, env=env)
+    first = decide('anthropic/haiku')
+    second = decide('openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna')
+    preference = state / 'model-preference.toml'
+    print('preference_before=MISSING')
+    print('first_rc=%s' % first.returncode)
+    print(first.stdout.strip())
+    print('second_rc=%s' % second.returncode)
+    print(second.stdout.strip())
+    print('preference_after=%s' % ('PRESENT' if preference.exists() else 'MISSING'))

+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path('/home/federico/SET-AGENTES')
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'ai/scripts'))
+import models_config
+import routing
+from routing_core.domain import CatalogSnapshot, StaticRoute
+
+ROSTER = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / 'roles.tsv')
+FULL_ROLES = ('implementer', 'package-reviewer')
+TOOLS = ('read', 'shell', 'write')
+
+def route(provider, model, tier, family, roles=FULL_ROLES, effort='medium', priority=1):
+    route_id = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, family, effort, (tier,), roles, TOOLS, priority)
+    return StaticRoute(2, provider, model, family, effort, tier, roles, TOOLS, priority, route_id)
+
+def facts(role, runtime, **changes):
+    value = dict(role=role, operation='change', task_class='documentation', read_write='write', write_started=False, risk='low', criticality='', affected_surfaces=(), required_tools=('read',), context_required=True, context_present=True, critical_coverage=True, selected_runtime=runtime)
+    value.update(changes)
+    return value
+
+def service(snapshot, inventory, path):
+    return routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, ROSTER, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(path / 'state'))
+
+def print_decision(name, decision):
+    print(name)
+    print('selected=%s/%s execution_enabled=%s' % (decision.provider, decision.model, decision.execution_enabled))
+    for reason in decision.reason_codes:
+        if reason.startswith('MODEL_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE'):
+            print(reason)
+
+def review_barrier(name, requested, candidate, alternate):
+    writer_route = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'frontier', 'opus')
+    identities = frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (writer_route, candidate, alternate))
+    snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((writer_route, candidate, alternate), identities)
+    inventory = {('opencode', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}, ('opencode', candidate.provider): {candidate.model}, ('opencode', alternate.provider): {alternate.model}}
+    inventory.setdefault(('opencode', 'anthropic'), set()).add('opus')
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+        svc = service(snapshot, inventory, Path(td))
+        writer = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode')))
+        svc.store.mark_dispatched(writer.run_id)
+        svc.store.close_run(writer.run_id, 'success')
+        review = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('package-reviewer', 'inspection', 'documentation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('package-reviewer', 'opencode', operation='inspection', read_write='read')), review_of_run_id=writer.run_id, model_request=requested)
+        print_decision(name, review)
+
+review_barrier('REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT', ('anthropic', 'sonnet'), route('anthropic', 'sonnet', 'frontier', 'sonnet'), route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+review_barrier('REVIEW_MODEL_CONFLICT', ('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8'), route('opencode-zen', 'claude-opus-4-8', 'frontier', 'claude-opus'), route('opencode-go', 'kimi-k3', 'frontier', 'kimi'))
+
+fast = route('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'fast', 'gpt-5.6', roles=('implementer',))
+balanced = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((fast, balanced), frozenset((r.route_id, 'opencode', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (fast, balanced)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('opencode', 'openai-codex'): {'gpt-5.6-sol'}, ('opencode', 'opencode-zen'): {'kimi-k2.7-code'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='opencode'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'opencode', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('openai-codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol'))
+    print_decision('TIER_INSUFFICIENT', decision)
+
+eligible = route('anthropic', 'opus', 'balanced', 'opus', roles=('implementer',))
+unaudited = route('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code', 'balanced', 'kimi', roles=('implementer',))
+snapshot = CatalogSnapshot((eligible, unaudited), frozenset((r.route_id, 'claude-code', r.provider, r.model, r.family, r.effort) for r in (eligible, unaudited)))
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = service(snapshot, {('claude-code', 'anthropic'): {'opus'}}, Path(td))
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'implementation', selected_runtime='claude-code'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'claude-code', task_class='implementation')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'kimi-k2.7-code'))
+    print_decision('UNAUDITED_PAIR', decision)
+
+config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / 'models.toml')
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    svc = routing._compose_for_tests(config, ROSTER, {('codex', 'openai-codex'): set(config['catalog']['codex'])}, Path(td) / 'state', simulate=False)
+    decision = svc.route(routing.TaskRequest('implementer', 'change', 'mechanical', selected_runtime='codex'), svc._observe_for_invocation(**facts('implementer', 'codex', task_class='mechanical')), model_request=('opencode-zen', 'definitely-not-a-curated-model-xyz'))
